{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_4","type":"command_execution","command":"/usr/local/bin/zsh -lc \"sed -n '1,280p' pbatch/config.py && sed -n '480,560p' pbatch/config.py && sed -n '1,460p' pbatch/tui_adapter_codex.py && sed -n '1,180p' tests/test_agent_adapter.py && sed -n '1,380p' tests/test_tui_adapter_codex.py\"","aggregated_output":"\"\"\"Declarative pi-batch defaults, adapters and validator registry.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import NamedTuple, Optional\n\nfrom .assets import (PACKAGE_ROOT, TOOL_ROOT, asset_candidates, expand_validator_registry,\n                     is_tool_asset, placeholder_values, resolve_asset_path)\nfrom .config_device import _bool_setting, load_device_fabric\nfrom .config_values import _choice_setting, _float_setting, _int_setting, _string_setting\nfrom .validator_schema import schema_validator_command\n\ntry:\n    import yaml\nexcept ImportError:\n    yaml = None\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    datefmt=\"%H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"pi-batch\")\n\n\n# Configuration documents are versioned independently from the package\n# version.  Missing ``config_version`` remains accepted as version 0 so old\n# projects keep working; a future version must fail closed instead of being\n# silently interpreted with today's defaults.\nCONFIG_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1\n\n\ndef _batch_config_candidates() -> list[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Return config candidates in the same order used by the loader.\"\"\"\n    candidates = []\n    script_dir = os.environ.get(\"PBATCH_SCRIPT_DIR\", \"\")\n    if script_dir:\n        candidates.append(Path(script_dir) / \"pi-batch.yaml\")\n    candidates.append(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / \"pi-batch.yaml\")\n    candidates.append(Path(\"pi-batch.yaml\"))\n    candidates.append(Path(TOOL_ROOT) / \"pi-batch.yaml\")\n    return candidates\n\n\ndef _batch_config_path() -> Optional[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Return the first existing project/tool config path, if any.\"\"\"\n    return next((path for path in _batch_config_candidates() if path.exists()), None)\n\n\ndef _validate_config_version(data: dict, path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject malformed or newer config documents before applying defaults.\"\"\"\n    version = data.get(\"config_version\", 0)\n    if isinstance(version, bool) or not isinstance(version, int):\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: config_version must be an integer\")\n    if version < 0:\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: config_version must be >= 0\")\n    if version > CONFIG_SCHEMA_VERSION:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"{path}: config_version {version} is newer than supported \"\n            f\"version {CONFIG_SCHEMA_VERSION}\")\n\n\ndef _find_batch_config() -> Optional[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Locate and parse pi-batch.yaml: the entry script directory\n    (PBATCH_SCRIPT_DIR, set by the pi-batch.py shim) wins, then the package\n    directory, then the process working directory. None when absent.\"\"\"\n    if not yaml:\n        return None\n    path = _batch_config_path()\n    if path is None:\n        return None\n    data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) or {}\n    if isinstance(data, dict):\n        _validate_config_version(data, path)\n        return data\n    return None\n\ndef _load_batch_config(path: str = \"pi-batch.yaml\") -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Optional defaults for pi-batch. Missing file -> {} (built-in defaults\n    below apply), so the tool still runs standalone with zero config -- copy\n    pi-batch.yaml alongside pi-batch.py to point it at a different agent\n    CLI.\"\"\"\n    data = _find_batch_config()\n    return data if data is not None else {}\n\ndef _find_user_config() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"用户级配置（分层低优先层；项目 pi-batch.yaml 覆盖之）。\"\"\"\n    from . import user_dirs\n    if not yaml:\n        return {}\n    path = user_dirs.user_settings_path()\n    if not path.exists():\n        return {}\n    try:\n        data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) or {}\n    except (OSError, ValueError):\n        log.warning(\"User settings %s unreadable; ignoring\", path)\n        return {}\n    if isinstance(data, dict):\n        _validate_config_version(data, path)\n    return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n\ndef _merge_config(project: dict, user: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"项目配置覆盖用户配置（节内键级合并）。\"\"\"\n    merged: dict = {}\n    for section_name in set(project) | set(user):\n        proj = project.get(section_name, {})\n        usr = user.get(section_name, {})\n        if isinstance(proj, dict) and isinstance(usr, dict):\n            merged[section_name] = {**usr, **proj}\n        else:\n            merged[section_name] = proj if section_name in project else usr\n    return merged\n\n_BATCH_CFG = _merge_config(_load_batch_config(), _find_user_config())\n\ndef _section(name: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Return a mapping config section, falling back on malformed input.\"\"\"\n    value = _BATCH_CFG.get(name, {})\n    if isinstance(value, dict):\n        return value\n    log.warning(\"Config section '%s' must be a mapping; using defaults\", name)\n    return {}\n\n_AGENT_CFG = _section(\"agent\")\n# Optional per-agent override sections: agent.agents.<bin>.* win over the\n# global agent.* settings for that binary (G line: claude/codex/gemini have\n# different failure signatures and session-flag syntax).\n_AGENTS_CFG = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"agents\")\nif not isinstance(_AGENTS_CFG, dict):\n    _AGENTS_CFG = {}\n\ndef _agent_section(bin_name: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Per-agent config section for a binary name (agent.agents.<bin>),\n    or {} when the section is absent/malformed.\"\"\"\n    if not bin_name:\n        return {}\n    section = _AGENTS_CFG.get(bin_name)\n    return section if isinstance(section, dict) else {}\n\n_DEFAULT_ARGUMENT_FLAGS = {\n    \"prompt\": (\"-p\",),\n    \"model\": (\"--model\",),\n    \"provider\": (\"--provider\",),\n    \"thinking\": (\"--thinking\",),\n    \"tools\": (\"--tools\",),\n    \"exclude_tools\": (\"--exclude-tools\",),\n}\n\n\ndef _argument_tokens(value, default: tuple, label: str) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"Normalize one adapter flag without shell-splitting untrusted config.\n\n    An empty string/list disables an optional flag. For ``prompt`` it means\n    the prompt is positional, which is required by CLIs such as Codex.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        return (value,) if value else ()\n    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) and all(\n            isinstance(token, str) and token for token in value):\n        return tuple(value)\n    log.warning(\"Config argument_flags.%s must be a string or string list; using defaults\",\n                label)\n    return default\n\n\ndef agent_extra_args(bin_name: str = \"\") -> tuple[str, ...]:\n    \"\"\"Per-agent trailing args (agent.agents.<bin>.extra_args, falling back\n    to the global agent.extra_args). Used by CLIs that need invocation-level\n    flags outside the six argument_flags slots — e.g. Codex's sandbox mode\n    (``codex exec --approve-for-me``). Empty tuple when absent/malformed.\n    \"\"\"\n    section = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN)\n    value = section.get(\"extra_args\", _AGENT_CFG.get(\"extra_args\"))\n    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) and all(\n            isinstance(token, str) and token for token in value):\n        return tuple(value)\n    if value not in (None, []):\n        log.warning(\n            \"agent extra_args must be a list of non-empty strings; ignored: %r\",\n            value)\n    return ()\n\n\ndef agent_argument_flags(bin_name: str = \"\") -> dict[str, tuple]:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the argv adapter for an agent binary.\n\n    Precedence is agent.agents.<bin>.argument_flags > agent.argument_flags >\n    the historical pi-compatible flag contract. Per-key merging keeps a\n    small override backwards compatible with the existing defaults.\n    \"\"\"\n    global_flags = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"argument_flags\")\n    global_flags = global_flags if isinstance(global_flags, dict) else {}\n    local_flags = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"argument_flags\")\n    local_flags = local_flags if isinstance(local_flags, dict) else {}\n    resolved = {}\n    for key, default in _DEFAULT_ARGUMENT_FLAGS.items():\n        value = local_flags.get(key, global_flags.get(key, default))\n        resolved[key] = _argument_tokens(value, default, key)\n    return resolved\n\n\ndef build_agent_argv(bin_name: str, prompt: str, *, model: str = \"\",\n                     provider: str = \"\", thinking: str = \"\", tools: str = \"\",\n                     exclude_tools: str = \"\",\n                     session_flags: Optional[list] = None) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Build an agent argv from the configured adapter, without a shell.\"\"\"\n    flags = agent_argument_flags(bin_name)\n    cmd = [bin_name, *flags[\"prompt\"], prompt]\n    for key, value in ((\"model\", model), (\"provider\", provider),\n                       (\"thinking\", thinking), (\"tools\", tools),\n                       (\"exclude_tools\", exclude_tools)):\n        if not value:\n            continue\n        if not flags[key]:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"agent adapter for {bin_name!r} does not support {key}\")\n        cmd.extend([*flags[key], value])\n    if session_flags:\n        cmd.extend(session_flags)\n    cmd.extend(agent_extra_args(bin_name))\n    return cmd\n\n\ndef agent_prompt_index(bin_name: str = \"\") -> int:\n    \"\"\"Index of the prompt value in argv built by :func:`build_agent_argv`.\"\"\"\n or agents start.\"\"\"\n    name = str(bin_name or \"\").strip()\n    if not name:\n        return [\"agent binary is empty\"]\n    errors = []\n    if shutil.which(name) is None:\n        errors.append(f\"agent executable not found or not executable: {name}\")\n    try:\n        agent_argument_flags(name)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n        errors.append(f\"invalid adapter for {name}: {exc}\")\n    return errors\n# Default provider/CLI failure signatures (G line). Per-agent overrides:\n#   agent.error_patterns: [...]      (all agents)\n#   agent.agents.<bin>.error_patterns: [...]   (one binary)\n# Empty list disables signature rejection for that agent.\n_DEFAULT_ERROR_PATTERNS = (\n    # provider error codes (quota, rate limit, billing, auth)\n    r\"rate_?limit_?error\",\n    r\"insufficient_?quota\",\n    r\"quota_?exceeded\",\n    r\"credit_?balance_?too_?low\",\n    r\"insufficient\\s+(?:balance|funds)\",\n    r\"billing_?error\",\n    r\"payment_?required\",\n    r\"invalid_?api_?key\",\n    r\"authentication_?error\",\n    r\"context_?length_?exceeded\",\n    r\"overloaded_?error\",\n    r\"429 too many requests\",\n    # network connectivity failures (offline, DNS, TLS, proxy)\n    r\"network is unreachable\",\n    r\"no route to host\",\n    r\"temporary failure in name resolution\",\n    r\"name or service not known\",\n    r\"dns resolution failed\",\n    r\"getaddrinfo\",\n    r\"max retries exceeded\",\n    r\"certificate verify failed\",\n    r\"connectionerror\",\n    r\"sslerror\",\n    r\"proxyerror\",\n    r\"econnrefused\",\n    r\"econnreset\",\n_DEVICE_FABRIC = load_device_fabric(_section(\"device_fabric\"))\nDEVICE_FABRIC_ENABLED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.enabled\nDEVICE_FABRIC_MODE = _DEVICE_FABRIC.mode\nDEVICE_FABRIC_LAN_SCAN = _DEVICE_FABRIC.lan_scan\nDEVICE_FABRIC_BENCHMARK = _DEVICE_FABRIC.benchmark\nDEVICE_FABRIC_AUTO_INSTALL = _DEVICE_FABRIC.auto_install\nDEVICE_FABRIC_APPROVAL_REQUIRED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.approval_required\nDEVICE_FABRIC_MTLS_REQUIRED = _DEVICE_FABRIC.mtls_required\nDEVICE_FABRIC_SHORT_LIVED_CREDENTIALS = _DEVICE_FABRIC.short_lived_credentials\nDEVICE_FABRIC_STATIC = _DEVICE_FABRIC.static\nDEVICE_FABRIC_CLUSTERS = _DEVICE_FABRIC.clusters\n\n# Task type classifier (pbatch/classifier.py): deterministic keyword gate\n# that runs BEFORE execution and routes frontend UI tasks to the UI\n# generation pipeline (--classify / `pi-batch.py classify`).\nCLASSIFIER_KEYWORDS = _string_setting(_CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"keywords\", \"\")\nCLASSIFIER_FRONTEND_PIPELINE = _string_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"frontend_pipeline\",\n    \"examples/frontend-implementation-pipeline.yaml\", allow_empty=False)\nCLASSIFIER_BACKEND_PIPELINE = _string_setting(\n    _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"backend_pipeline\",\n    \"examples/backend-implementation-pipeline.yaml\", allow_empty=False)\n# Minimum top score for a confident classification (2 = one strong hit).\nCLASSIFIER_MIN_SCORE = _int_setting(_CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"min_score\", 2, 1)\n# Route when this share of a batch's tasks classify as frontend UI.\nCLASSIFIER_FRONTEND_RATIO = _float_setting(\n _CLASSIFIER_CFG, \"tie_policy\", \"frontend\", (\"plain\", \"frontend\", \"backend\"))\n\n_DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS = {\n    \"start\": [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\", \"--name\", \"{name}\"],\n    \"continue\": [\"--session-id\", \"{session}\"],\n    \"fork\": [\"--fork\", \"{session}\"],\n}\n\ndef agent_session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str,\n                        bin_name: str = \"\") -> list:\n    \"\"\"Resolve session flags for a call (start/continue/fork), replacing\n    {session} and {name} placeholders. Per-agent override\n    (agent.agents.<bin>.session_flags) wins over agent.session_flags,\n    then pi-style defaults — so a non-pi agent CLI can point these at its\n    own continue-session flags without touching shared config (G line).\"\"\"\n    configured = _agent_section(bin_name or AGENT_BIN).get(\"session_flags\")\n    if configured is None:\n        configured = _AGENT_CFG.get(\"session_flags\")\n    cfg = configured if isinstance(configured, dict) else {}\n    flags = cfg.get(key)\n    if (not isinstance(flags, (list, tuple)) or not flags\n            or not all(isinstance(flag, str) for flag in flags)):\n        if flags is not None:\n            log.warning(\"Config session_flags.%s must be a string list; using defaults\", key)\n        flags = _DEFAULT_SESSION_FLAGS[key]\n    return [f.replace(\"{session}\", session_id).replace(\"{name}\", session_name) for f in flags]\n\ndef _session_flags(key: str, session_id: str, session_name: str) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Backward-compatible alias resolving flags for the configured bin.\"\"\"\n    return agent_session_flags(key, session_id, session_name)\n\ndef _load_validators() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Read validators from the already merged project/user configuration.\n\n    The rest of this module resolves settings from ``_BATCH_CFG``. PROJECT_BACKEND_ROOT, PROJECT_REPORT_ROOT)\n\nVALIDATORS = _load_validators()\n\nclass ValidatorSpec(NamedTuple):\n    \"\"\"One resolved validator entry.\n\n    cmd:   shell command ({output}/{cwd} placeholders)\n\"\"\"把 Codex ``exec --json`` JSONL 映射为统一 TUI Runtime 事件。\n未知 provider item 发出有界占位；正文不提升为审批、Gate、Review 或命令事件。\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nfrom collections import deque\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport queue\nimport signal\nimport subprocess\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom typing import Any, Callable, MAX_FIELD_BYTES = 256, 48 * 1024\nTRUNCATION_MARKER, _IGNORED_STDERR = \"\\n...[truncated]\", \"Reading additional input from stdin...\"\n_ITEM_TYPES = frozenset({\"agent_message\", \"command_execution\", \"file_change\", \"todo_list\"})\nSpawn = Callable[[Sequence[str], str], Any]\nclass _EventQueue:\n    \"\"\"有界可关闭队列；压力下只牺牲 warning，重要事件使用背压。\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, maxsize: int):\n        if isinstance(maxsize, bool) or not isinstance(maxsize, int) or maxsize <= 0:\n            raise ValueError(\"maxsize must be a positive integer\")\n        self.maxsize = maxsize\n        self._items: deque[RuntimeEvent] = deque()\n        self._closed = False\n        self._condition = threading.Condition()\n    def put(self, event: RuntimeEvent) -> tuple[bool, bool]:\n        with self._condition:\n            dropped = False\n            while len(self._items) >= self.maxsize and not self._closed:\n                warning = next((index for index, queued in enumerate(self._items)\n                                if queued.kind == \"runtime.warning\"), None)\n                if warning is not None:\n                    del self._items[warning]\n                    dropped = True\n                    break\n                if event.kind == \"runtime.warning\":\n                    return False, True\n                self._condition.wait()\n            if self._closed:\n                return False, dropped\n            self._items.append(event)\n            self._condition.notify_all()\n            return True, dropped\n    def get(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> RuntimeEvent | None:\n        with self._condition:\n            deadline = None if timeout is None else time.monotonic() + max(0, timeout)\n            while not self._items and not self._closed:\n                remaining = None if deadline is None else deadline - time.monotonic()\n                if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:\n                    raise queue.Empty\n                self._condition.wait(remaining)\n            if not self._items:\n                return None\n            event = self._items.popleft()\n            self._condition.notify_all()\n            return event\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        with self._condition:\n            self._closed = True\n            self._condition.notify_all()\nclass CodexExecAdapter:\n    \"\"\"执行一次 Codex JSONL turn，并投影为 provider-neutral 事件。\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, cwd: str | os.PathLike[str], *,\n                 argv_prefix: Sequence[str] = for part in prefix):\n            raise ValueError(\"argv_prefix must contain non-empty arguments\")\n        self._cwd, self._argv_prefix = os.fspath(cwd), prefix\n        self._spawn, self._queue = self._started_monotonic = 0.0\n        self._info = _runtime_info()\n    @property\n    def session(self) -> RuntimeSession:\n        return self._session\n    def initialize(self, request: Any = None) -> RuntimeInfo:\n        self._ensure_open()\n        workspace_id = str(_request_value(request, \"workspace_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n        if workspace_id:\n UnsupportedOperation(\"start_session\", \"session_resume\")\n    def resume_session(self, request: Any) -> RuntimeSession:\n        raise UnsupportedOperation(\"resume_session\", \"session_resume\")\n start_turn(self, request: StartTurnRequest | str | Mapping[str, Any], *,\n                   argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> TurnHandle:\n        self._ensure_open()\n        if not self._initialized:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"initialize must be called before start_turn\")\n        default = request if isinstance(request, str) else \"\"\n        prompt = = self._argv_prefix if argv is None else argv\n        command = tuple(str(part) for part in base) + (prompt,)\n        process = self._spawn(command, self._cwd)\n        with self._lock:\n            self._active, self._process = handle, process\n            self._terminal = self._interrupt_requested = self._overflow_reported = False\n            self._usage, self._protocol_errors, self._lock:\n            if self._active is not None and not self._active.done:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"a codex exec turn is already running\")\n            requested = str(_request_value(request, \"session_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n            if requested and requested != self._session.session_id:\n                raise ValueError(\"turn request does not belong to the active session\")\n        return TurnHandle(\n            session_id=self._session.session_id,\n            run_id=str(_request_value(request, \"run_id\", \"\") or _id(\"run\")),\n            turn_id=str(_request_value(request, \"turn_id\", \"\") or _id(\"turn\")),\n            actor_id=str(_request_value(request, \"actor_id\", \"agent_main\") or\n                         \"agent_main\"), started_at=_now())\n    def _start_workers(self, handle: TurnHandle, process: Any) -> None:\n        stdout = threading.Thread(target=self._read_stdout, args=(handle, process),\n                                  name=f\"codex-out-{handle.turn_id}\", daemon=True)\n        stderr = threading.Thread(target=self._read_stderr, args=(handle, process),\n                                  name=f\"codex-err-{handle.turn_id}\", daemon=True)\n        monitor = threading.Thread(\n            target=self._monitor, args=(handle, process, stdout, stderr),\n            name=f\"codex-wait-{handle.turn_id}\", daemon=True)\n        self._threads = [stdout, stderr, monitor]\n        stdout.start()\n        stderr.start()\n        monitor.start()\n    def _read_stdout(self, handle: TurnHandle, process: Any) -> None:\n        for source_line in getattr(process, \"stdout\", ()) or ():\n            raw = str(source_line).rstrip(\"\\r\\n\")\n            try:\n                value = json.loads(raw)\n            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                self._warn_protocol(handle, raw)\n                continue\n            if not isinstance(value, Mapping):\n                self._warn_protocol(handle, raw)\n                continue\n            self._dispatch(handle, dict(value), raw)\n    def _warn_protocol(self, handle: TurnHandle, raw: str) -> None:\n        self._protocol_errors += 1\n        self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n        self._publish(handle, \"runtime.warning\", {\n            \"stream\": \"stdout\", \"category\": \"protocol_error\",\n            \"protocol_error_count\": self._protocol_errors, **_text(\"raw\", raw)})\n    def _read_stderr(self, handle: TurnHandle, process: Any) -> None:\n        for source_line in getattr(process, \"stderr\", ()) or ():\n            raw = str(source_line).strip()\n            if not raw or raw == _IGNORED_STDERR:\n                continue\n            self._session_ready.wait(0.05)\n            self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n            self._publish(handle, \"runtime.warning\",\n                          {\"stream\": \"stderr\", **_text(\"text\", raw)})\n    def _dispatch(self, handle: TurnHandle, value: dict[str, Any], raw: str) -> None:\n        provider_type = str(value.get(\"type\", \"\"))\n        if provider_type == \"thread.started\":\n            self._session.provider_session_id = _bound(value.get(\"thread_id\", \"\"), 2048)[0]\n            self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n            return\n        self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n        if self._is_terminal(handle):\n            return\n        if provider_type == \"turn.started\":\n            self._start_provider_turn(handle)\n        elif provider_type in {\"item.started\", \"item.completed\", \"item.updated\"}:\n            self._dispatch_item(handle, provider_type, value.get(\"item\"), raw)\n        elif provider_type == \"turn.completed\":\n            self._complete_provider_turn(handle, value.get(\"usage\"))\n            return\n        elif provider_type == \"turn.failed\":\n            self._finish(handle, \"turn.failed\", TurnState.FAILED,\n                         {**_text(\"reason\", value.get(\"error\", \"\"))})\n            return\n        else:\n            self._unknown(handle, provider_type, raw)\n        if \"usage\" in value:\n            self._emit_usage(handle, value.get(\"usage\"))\n    def _start_provider_turn(self, handle: TurnHandle) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._terminal or handle.state != TurnState.QUEUED: return\n        self._publish(handle, \"turn.queued\", {\"turn_id\": handle.turn_id, \"state\": TurnState.QUEUED})\n        with self._lock:\n provider_type.removeprefix(\"item.\")\n        item_type = str(item.get(\"type\", \"\"))\n        if item_type not in _ITEM_TYPES:\n            self._unknown(handle, item_type or provider_type, raw)\n        elif item_type == \"agent_message\":\n            self._message_item(handle, phase, item)\n        elif item_type == \"command_execution\":\n            self._command_item(handle, phase, handle: TurnHandle, phase: str,\n                      item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n        item_id = _bound(item.get(\"id\", \"\"), 2048)[0]\n        if phase == \"started\":\n            self._publish(handle, \"message.started\", {\"item_id\": item_id})\n        else:\n            self._publish(handle, \"message.delta\",\n                          {\"item_id\": item_id, **_text(\"text\", item.get(\"text\", \"\"))})\n    def _command_item(self, handle: TurnHandle, phase: str,\n                      item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n        base = {\"item_id\": _bound(item.get(\"id\", \"\"), 2048)[0],\n                \"command\": _bound(item.get(\"command\", \"\"), 24 * 1024)[0],\n                \"status\": _bound(item.get(\"status\", \"\"), 256)[0]}\n base[\"item_id\"],\n            **_text(\"aggregated_output\", item.get(\"aggregated_output\", \"\"))})\n        exit_code = item.get(\"exit_code\")\n        safe_code = exit_code if isinstance(exit_code, int) and not isinstance(\n            exit_code, bool) else None\n        self._publish(handle, \"command.completed\", {**base, \"exit_code\": safe_code})\n    def _file_item(self, handle: TurnHandle, phase: str,\n                   item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n        changes, truncated = _bounded_changes(item.get(\"changes\"))\n        self._publish(handle, \"file.changed\", {\n            \"item_id\": _bound(item.get(\"id\", \"\"), 2048)[0],\n            \"status\": _bound(item.get(\"status\", phase), 256)[0],\n            \"changes\": changes, \"truncated\": truncated})\n    def _plan_item(self, handle: TurnHandle, phase: str,\n                   item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n        items, truncated = _bounded_list(item.get(\"items\", item.get(\"todos\", [])))\n        self._publish(handle, \"plan.updated\", {\n            \"item_id\": _bound(item.get(\"id\", \"\"), 2048)[0],\n            \"status\": _bound(item.get(\"status\", phase), 256)[0],\n            \"items\": items, \"truncated\": truncated})\n    def _emit_usage(self, handle: TurnHandle, usage: Any) -> None:\n        if isinstance(usage, Mapping):\n            for name in (\"input_tokens\", \"cached_input_tokens\", \"cache_write_input_tokens\", \"output_tokens\", \"reasoning_output_tokens\"):\n                if _number(value := usage.get(name)):\n                    self._usage[name] = value\n        self._publish(handle, \"usage.updated\", {\"usage\": dict(self._usage)})\n    def _complete_provider_turn(self, handle: TurnHandle, usage: Any) -> None:\n        self._emit_usage(handle, usage)\n        elapsed_ms = max(0, int((time.monotonic() - self._started_monotonic) * 1000))\n        self._finish(handle, \"turn.completed\", TurnState.SUCCEEDED, {\n            \"elapsed_ms\": elapsed_ms, \"usage\": dict(self._usage)})\n    def _unknown(self, handle: TurnHandle, def _ensure_session_event(self, handle: TurnHandle) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._session_announced:\n                return\n            self._session_announced = True\n threading.Thread,\n                 stderr: threading.Thread) -> None:\n        stdout.join()\n        stderr.join()\n        try:\n            returncode = process.wait()\n        except Exception as exc:\n            returncode = -1\n            self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n            self._publish(handle, \"runtime.error\",\n                          {\"phase\": \"wait\", **_text(\"reason\", exc)})\n        self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n        if self._is_terminal(handle):\n            return\n        if self._interrupt_requested:\n            self._finish(handle, \"turn.interrupted\", TurnState.INTERRUPTED,\n                         {\"returncode\": returncode})\n            return\n        state = TurnState.FAILED if returncode else _finish(self, handle: TurnHandle, kind: str, state: str,\n                payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._terminal or handle.done:\n                return\n            self._terminal, handle.state = True, state\n        self._publish(handle, kind,\n                      {\"state\": state, \"turn_id\": handle.turn_id, **payload})\n        handle._done.set()\n    def _is_terminal(self, handle: TurnHandle) -> bool:\n        with self._lock:\n            return self._terminal or handle.done\n    def interrupt_turn(self, request: Any) -> None:\n        \"\"\"回收当前进程组后提交不可回退的 interrupted 终态。\"\"\"\n        self._ensure_open()\n        default = request if isinstance(request, str) else \"\"\n        turn_id = str(_request_value(request, \"turn_id\", default))\n        with self._lock:\n            handle = self._active\n            if handle is None or handle.turn_id != turn_id:\n                raise ValueError(\"interrupt request does not match the active turn\")\n            if self._interrupt_requested:\n                return\n            if self._terminal or handle.done:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"cannot interrupt a terminal turn\")\n            self._interrupt_requested, handle.state = True, TurnState.INTERRUPTING\n            process = self._process\n        self._ensure_session_event(handle)\n        self._publish(handle, \"turn.interrupting\", {\"state\": handle.state})\n        _stop_process(process)\n        self._finish(handle, \"turn.interrupted\", TurnState.INTERRUPTED, {})\n    def events(self) -> Iterator[RuntimeEvent]:\n        \"\"\"阻塞消费事件；close 且队列耗尽后自然 StopIteration。\"\"\"\n        while True:\n            event = self._queue.get()\n            if event is None:\n                return\n            yield event\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"幂等回收进程组、读线程与事件队列。\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._closed:\n                return\n            self._closed = True\n            process, handle = self._process, self._active\n        _stop_process(process)\n        for worker in list(self._threads):\n            if worker is not return\n                seq = self._seq_by_run.get(handle.run_id, 0) + 1\n                self._seq_by_run[handle.run_id] = seq\n            event = RuntimeEvent(\n                event_id=_id(\"evt\"), seq=seq, occurred_at=_now(),\n                workspace_id=self._session.workspace_id,\n                session_id=self._session.session_id, run_id=handle.run_id,\n def __init__(self, stdout=(), stderr=(), returncode=0):\n        self.stdout = list(stdout)\n        self.stderr = list(stderr)\n        self.returncode = returncode\n        self.terminate_called = False\n        self.kill_called = False\n        self._waited = False\n\n    def poll(self):\n        return self.returncode if self._waited else None\n\n    def terminate(self):\n        self.terminate_called = True\n\n    def kill(self):\n        self.kill_called = True\n        self.returncode = -9\n        self._waited = True\n\n    def wait(self, timeout=None):\n        self._waited = True\n        return self.returncode\n\n\nclass _BlockingLines:\n    def __init__(self, released: threading.Event):\n        self._released = released\n\n    def __iter__(self):\n        return self\n\n    def __next__(self):\n        self._released.wait(2)\n        raise StopIteration\n\n\nclass BlockingProc(FakeProc):\n    \"\"\"只有 kill 后 stdout 与 wait 才结束的假进程。\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.released = threading.Event()\n        super().__init__()\n        self.stdout = _BlockingLines(self.released)\n\n    def kill(self):\n        super().kill()\n        self.released.set()\n\n    def wait(self, timeout=None):\n        if not self.released.wait(timeout):\n            raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(\"codex\", timeout)\n        return \"terminate\")\n   425\t        if _wait(process):\n   426\t            return\n   427\t        try:\n   428\t            os.killpg(os.getpgid(pid), signal.SIGKILL)\n   429\t        except OSError:\n try:\n   436\t        return process.poll() is not None\n   437\t    except (AttributeError, OSError):\n   438\t        return False\n   439\tdef _wait(process: Any) -> bool:\n   440\t    try:\n   441\t        process.wait(timeout=0.25)\n   442\t        return True\n   443\t    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, TimeoutError, AttributeError, OSError):\n   444\t        return False\n   445\tdef _call(process: Any, method: str) -> None:\n   446\t    try:\n   447\t        getattr(process, method)()\n   448\t    except (AttributeError, OSError):\n   449\t        pass\n   450\tdef _bound(value: Any, maximum: int = MAX_FIELD_BYTES) -> tuple[str, bool, int]:\n   451\t    text = str(value if value is not None else \"\")\n   452\t    encoded, marker = text.encode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"), TRUNCATION_MARKER.encode()\n   453\t    if len(encoded) <= maximum:\n   454\t        return text, False, len(encoded)\n   455\t    prefix = encoded[:maximum - len(marker)].decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n   456\t    return prefix + TRUNCATION_MARKER, True, len(encoded)\n   457\tdef _text(name: str, value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:\n   458\t    text, truncated, original = _bound(value)\n   459\t    result: dict[str, Any] = {name: text, \"truncated\": truncated}\n   460\t    if truncated:\n   461\t        result[\"original_bytes\"] = original\n   462\t    return result\n   463\tdef _bounded_changes(value: Any) -> tuple[list[dict[str, str]], bool]:\n   464\t    if not isinstance(value, list):\n   465\t        return [], bool(value)\n   466\t    result: list[dict[str, str]] = []; truncated = False\n   467\t    for change in value:\n   468\t        if not isinstance(change, Mapping):\n   469\t            continue\n   470\t        path, path_cut, _ = _bound(change.get(\"path\", \"\"):\n    30\t        self.operation = operation\n    31\t        self.capability = capability\n    32\t        suffix = f\" (requires {capability})\" if capability else \"\"\n    33\t   100\t    state: str = TurnState.QUEUED\n   101\t    _done: threading.Event = field(default_factory=threading.Event,\n   102\t                                   repr=False, compare=False)\n   103\t\n self._done.wait(timeout)\n   107\t\n   108\t    @property\n   109\t    def done(self) -> bool:\n   110\t        return self._done.is_set()\n   111\t\n   112\t\n   113\t@runtime_checkable\n 2\t\n     3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     4\t\n     5\timport json\n     6\timport subprocess\n     7\timport threading\n     8\timport time\n     9\t\n    10\timport pytest\n    11\t\n    12\tfrom pbatch.tui_adapter_codex import CodexExecAdapter\n    13\tfrom pbatch.tui_runtime import (\n    14\t    ApprovalDecision,\n    15\t    InitializeRequest,\n    16\t    InterruptRequest,\n    17\t    ResumeSessionRequest,\n    18\t    StartSessionRequest,\n    19\t    StartTurnRequest,\n    20\t    SteerRequest,\n    21\t    UnsupportedOperation,\n    22\t)\n    23\t\n    24\t\n    25\tTHREAD_ID = \"01a0272d-66ab-74f1-8891-1013ac2cb87a\"\n    26\t\n    27\t\n    28\tdef _line(value) -> str:\n    29\t    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\"\n    30\t\n    31\t\n    32\tclass FakeProc:\n    33\t    \"\"\"提供 Popen 形状且可确定结束的内存进程。\"\"\"\n    34\t\n    35\t    def __init__(self, stdout=(), stderr=(), returncode=0):\n    36\t        self.stdout = list(stdout)\n    37\t        self.stderr = list(stderr)\n    38\t        self.returncode = returncode\n    39\t        self.terminate_called = False\n    40\t        self.kill_called = False\n    41\t        self._waited = False\n    42\t\n    43\t    def poll(self):\n    44\t        return self.returncode if self._waited else None\n    45\t\n    46\t    def terminate(self):\n    47\t        self.terminate_called = True\n    48\t\n    49\t    def kill(self):\n    50\t        self.kill_called = True\n    51\t        self.returncode = -9\n    52\t        self._waited = True\n    53\t\n    54\t    def wait(self, timeout=None):\n    55\t        self._waited = True\n    56\t        return self.returncode\n    57\t\n    58\t\n    59\tclass _BlockingLines:\n    60\t    def __init__(self, released: threading.Event):\n    61\t        self._released = released\n    62\t\n    63\t    def __iter__(self):\n    64\t        return self\n    65\t\n    66\t    def __next__(self):\n    67\t        self._released.wait(2)\n    68\t        raise StopIteration\n    69\t\n    70\t\n    71\tclass BlockingProc(FakeProc):\n    72\t    \"\"\"只有 kill 后 stdout 与 wait 才结束的假进程。\"\"\"\n    73\t\n    74\t    def __init__(self):\n    75\t        self.released = threading.Event()\n    76\t        super().__init__()\n    77\t        self.stdout = _BlockingLines(self.released)\n    78\t\n    79\t    def kill(self):\n    80\t        super().kill()\n    81\t        self.released.set()\n    82\t\n    83\t    def wait(self, timeout=None):\n    84\t        if not self.released.wait(timeout):\n    85\t            raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(\"codex\", timeout)\n    86\t        return self.returncode\n    87\t\n    88\t\n    89\tclass FakeSpawn:\n    90\t    \"\"\"记录 argv/cwd 并返回测试预置进程。\"\"\"\n    91\t\n    92\t    def __init__(self, process: FakeProc):\n    93\t        self.process = process\n    94\t        self.calls = []\n    95\t\n    96\t    def __call__(self, argv, cwd):\n    97\t        self.calls.append((tuple(argv), cwd))\n    98\t        return self.process\n    99\t\n   100\t\n   101\tdef _run(lines, *, stderr=(), maxsize=256):\n   102\t    process = FakeProc(lines, stderr)\n \"text\": \"the reply\"}}),\n   121\t        _line({\"type\": \"item.started\", \"item\": {\n   122\t            \"id\": \"item_1\", \"type\": \"command_execution\", \"command\": \"ls\",\n   123\t Used to detect session\n     5\tgrowth (rotation watermark) and compaction entries (warn), and to build\n     6\tthe --fork invocation that starts a fresh session from the old file.\n     7\tEverything degrades gracefully: a missing/unreadable session simply means\n     8\t\"no rotation needed\".\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    12\t\n    13\timport json\n    14\timport os\n    15\tfrom glob import escape as glob_escape\n    16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n    17\tfrom typing import Optional\n    18\t\n    19\tfrom . import config\n    20\tfrom .memory_io import bounded_lines\n    21\t\n    22\t# test-injectable (monkeypatch), default matches pi's runtime layout\n    23\tSESSIONS_DIR = Path.home() / \".pi\" / \"agent\" / \"sessions\"\n    24\t\n    25\t\n    26\tdef workdir_key(cwd: str) -> str:\n    27\t    \"\"\"pi's session-dir key for a workdir: the absolute path with its\n    28\t    leading '/' stripped and remaining '/' -> '-', wrapped in '--'. pi\n    29\t    writes `--home-u1-project--` for /home/u1/project; dashing the leading\n    30\t    slash too produces `---home-u1-project--`, which never matches a real\n    31\t    pi session tree (round-4 finding P0-2).\"\"\"\n    32\t    path = (cwd or os.getcwd()).lstrip(\"/\")\n    33\t    return \"--\" + path.replace(\"/\", \"-\") + \"--\"\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tdef session_file(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Newest session file for an id/name reference under *cwd*.\n    38\t\n    39\t    Pi normally uses the session id as the filename suffix, while older\n    40\t    adapters used the display name; accepting both keeps rotation portable.\n    41\t    \"\"\"\n    42\t    d = SESSIONS_DIR / workdir_key(cwd)\n    43\t    if not d.is_dir():\n    44\t        return None\n    45\t    try:\n    46\t        escaped = glob_escape(session_name)\n    47\t        candidates = sorted({path for pattern in (f\"*_{escaped}.jsonl\",\n    48\t                                                   f\"*_{escaped}-*.jsonl\")\n    49\t                             for path in d.glob(pattern)})\n    50\t    except OSError:\n    51\t        return None\n    52\t    return candidates[-1] if candidates else None\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tdef session_size_bytes(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> int:\n    56\t    \"\"\"Size of the active session file (0 when absent).\"\"\"\n    57\t    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    58\t    if f is None:\n    59\t        return 0\n    60\t    try:\n    61\t        return f.stat().st_size\n    62\t    except OSError:\n    63\t        return 0\n    64\t\n    65\t\n    66\tdef has_compaction(cwd: str, session_name: str) -> bool:\n    67\t    \"\"\"True when the session file contains a compaction entry (pi shrinks\n    68\t    the context; worth warning the operator about).\"\"\"\n    69\t    f = session_file(cwd, session_name)\n    70\t    if f is None:\n    71\t        return False\n    72\t    try:\n    73\t        for line in bounded_lines(f, config.SESSION_LINE_MAX_BYTES):\n    74\t            if line is None:\n    75\t                continue\n    76\t            try:\n    77\t                if json.loads(line).get(\"type\") == \"compaction\":\n    78\t                    return True\n    79\t            except ValueError:\n    80\t                continue\n    81\t    except OSError:\n    82\t        return False\n    83\t    return False\n    84\t\n    85\t\n    86\tdef fork_flags(session_file_path: str) -> list:\n    87\t    \"\"\"Agent flags that fork the session file into a fresh session (the\n    88\t    rotation mechanism; --fork is documented by pi 0.83).\"\"\"\n    89\t    from .config import _session_flags\n    90\t    return _session_flags(\"fork\", session_file_path, \"\")\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

def new_session(self, name=\"\"):\n        self.new_sessions.append(name)\n        return {\"session_name\": name or \"test-new\"}\n\n    def cancel(self):\n        self.cancel_calls += 1\n        return True\n\n\nclass BlockingBackend(FakeBackend):\n    def __init__(self, emit, **options):\n        super().__init__(emit, **options)\n        self.started = threading.Event()\n self.emit(\"stdout\", \"released\")\n        return {\"success\": True, \"stdout\": \"released\"}\n\n    def cancel(self):\n        self.cancel_calls += 1\n        self.release.set()\n        return
